# Virtual Desktop GUI to MCP++ Taskboard (VDG)

Consumable by `ipfs_accelerate_py.agent_supervisor` with task prefix `## VDG-`.

Companion objectives:
`implementation_plan/docs/50-virtual-desktop-gui-mcp.objectives.md`.

Runtime profile:
`config/virtual_desktop_gui_mcp_supervisor.json`.

Lane inventory:
`config/virtual_desktop_gui_mcp_lane_inventory.json`.

Program decision: **GO**.

## Parallel execution contract

- A task is selectable only when every `Depends on` task is completed.
- Ready tasks whose `Predicted files` overlap fail closed.
- Only lane `vdg-planning` edits this file or the objectives file.
- Workers do not push. Merge target is `agent/virtual-desktop-gui-mcp`.
- The watchdog cannot mark a task complete.

## Execution waves

| Wave | Tasks | Parallelism |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | 000, 001 | Operator, then seal |
| 1 | 010, 011, 050 | Inventory, census, and watchdog repair |
| 2 | 012 then 013; 051 then 052 | Join and harness; unstall follow-ups |
| 3 | 031, 032, 033, 034, 035, 036 | Six app families |
| 4 | 040 then 060 | Gate, then closeout |
